    def _check_command_response(
        response: _DocumentOut,
        max_wire_version: Optional[int],
        allowable_errors: Optional[Container[Union[int, str]]] = None,
        parse_write_concern_error: bool = False,
        pool_opts: Optional[PoolOptions] = None,
    ) -> None:
        """Check the response to a command for errors."""
        if "ok" not in response:
            # Server didn't recognize our message as a command.
            raise OperationFailure(
                response.get("$err"),  # type: ignore[arg-type]
                response.get("code"),
                response,
                max_wire_version,
            )
    
        if parse_write_concern_error and "writeConcernError" in response:
            _error = response["writeConcernError"]
            _labels = response.get("errorLabels")
            if _labels:
                _error.update({"errorLabels": _labels})
            _raise_write_concern_error(_error)
    
        if response["ok"]:
            return
    
        details = response
        errmsg = details["errmsg"]
        code = details.get("code")
    
        # For allowable errors, only check for error messages when the code is not
        # included.
        if allowable_errors:
            if code is not None:
                if code in allowable_errors:
                    return
            elif errmsg in allowable_errors:
                return
    
        # Server is "not primary" or "recovering"
        if code is not None:
            if code in _NOT_PRIMARY_CODES:
                raise NotPrimaryError(errmsg, response)
        elif HelloCompat.LEGACY_ERROR in errmsg or "node is recovering" in errmsg:
            raise NotPrimaryError(errmsg, response)
    
        # Other errors
        # findAndModify with upsert can raise duplicate key error
        if code in (11000, 11001, 12582):
            raise DuplicateKeyError(errmsg, code, response, max_wire_version)
        elif code == 50:
            # Append timeout details to MaxTimeMSExpired responses.
            if pool_opts:
                timeout_details = _get_timeout_details(pool_opts)
                errmsg += format_timeout_details(timeout_details)
            raise ExecutionTimeout(errmsg, code, response, max_wire_version)
        elif code == 43:
            raise CursorNotFound(errmsg, code, response, max_wire_version)

response = {'ok': 0.0, 'errmsg': 'Generic S3 error: Error performing PUT http://127.0.0.1:9000/ctl-bigspeed-bd042809/processes/bi...25bab4ad464b049177c95eb6ebf374d3b3fd1af9251148b658df7ac2e3e8</HostId></Error>', 'code': 1, 'codeName': 'InternalError'}
max_wire_version = 21, allowable_errors = None, parse_write_concern_error = True
pool_opts = None

    def _check_command_response(
        response: _DocumentOut,
        max_wire_version: Optional[int],
        allowable_errors: Optional[Container[Union[int, str]]] = None,
        parse_write_concern_error: bool = False,
        pool_opts: Optional[PoolOptions] = None,
    ) -> None:
        """Check the response to a command for errors."""
        if "ok" not in response:
            # Server didn't recognize our message as a command.
            raise OperationFailure(
                response.get("$err"),  # type: ignore[arg-type]
                response.get("code"),
                response,
                max_wire_version,
            )
    
        if parse_write_concern_error and "writeConcernError" in response:
            _error = response["writeConcernError"]
            _labels = response.get("errorLabels")
            if _labels:
                _error.update({"errorLabels": _labels})
            _raise_write_concern_error(_error)
    
        if response["ok"]:
            return
    
        details = response
        errmsg = details["errmsg"]
        code = details.get("code")
    
        # For allowable errors, only check for error messages when the code is not
        # included.
        if allowable_errors:
            if code is not None:
                if code in allowable_errors:
                    return
            elif errmsg in allowable_errors:
                return
    
        # Server is "not primary" or "recovering"
        if code is not None:
            if code in _NOT_PRIMARY_CODES:
                raise NotPrimaryError(errmsg, response)
        elif HelloCompat.LEGACY_ERROR in errmsg or "node is recovering" in errmsg:
            raise NotPrimaryError(errmsg, response)
    
        # Other errors
        # findAndModify with upsert can raise duplicate key error
        if code in (11000, 11001, 12582):
            raise DuplicateKeyError(errmsg, code, response, max_wire_version)
        elif code == 50:
            # Append timeout details to MaxTimeMSExpired responses.
            if pool_opts:
                timeout_details = _get_timeout_details(pool_opts)
                errmsg += format_timeout_details(timeout_details)
            raise ExecutionTimeout(errmsg, code, response, max_wire_version)
        elif code == 43:
            raise CursorNotFound(errmsg, code, response, max_wire_version)

    def _check_command_response(
        response: _DocumentOut,
        max_wire_version: Optional[int],
        allowable_errors: Optional[Container[Union[int, str]]] = None,
        parse_write_concern_error: bool = False,
        pool_opts: Optional[PoolOptions] = None,
    ) -> None:
        """Check the response to a command for errors."""
        if "ok" not in response:
            # Server didn't recognize our message as a command.
            raise OperationFailure(
                response.get("$err"),  # type: ignore[arg-type]
                response.get("code"),
                response,
                max_wire_version,
            )
    
        if parse_write_concern_error and "writeConcernError" in response:
            _error = response["writeConcernError"]
            _labels = response.get("errorLabels")
            if _labels:
                _error.update({"errorLabels": _labels})
            _raise_write_concern_error(_error)
    
        if response["ok"]:
            return
    
        details = response
        errmsg = details["errmsg"]
        code = details.get("code")
    
        # For allowable errors, only check for error messages when the code is not
        # included.
        if allowable_errors:
            if code is not None:
                if code in allowable_errors:
                    return
            elif errmsg in allowable_errors:
                return
    
        # Server is "not primary" or "recovering"
        if code is not None:
            if code in _NOT_PRIMARY_CODES:
                raise NotPrimaryError(errmsg, response)
        elif HelloCompat.LEGACY_ERROR in errmsg or "node is recovering" in errmsg:
            raise NotPrimaryError(errmsg, response)
    
        # Other errors
        # findAndModify with upsert can raise duplicate key error
        if code in (11000, 11001, 12582):
            raise DuplicateKeyError(errmsg, code, response, max_wire_version)
        elif code == 50:
            # Append timeout details to MaxTimeMSExpired responses.
            if pool_opts:
                timeout_details = _get_timeout_details(pool_opts)
                errmsg += format_timeout_details(timeout_details)
            raise ExecutionTimeout(errmsg, code, response, max_wire_version)
        elif code == 43:
            raise CursorNotFound(errmsg, code, response, max_wire_version)

    def _check_command_response(
        response: _DocumentOut,
        max_wire_version: Optional[int],
        allowable_errors: Optional[Container[Union[int, str]]] = None,
        parse_write_concern_error: bool = False,
        pool_opts: Optional[PoolOptions] = None,
    ) -> None:
        """Check the response to a command for errors."""
        if "ok" not in response:
            # Server didn't recognize our message as a command.
            raise OperationFailure(
                response.get("$err"),  # type: ignore[arg-type]
                response.get("code"),
                response,
                max_wire_version,
            )
    
        if parse_write_concern_error and "writeConcernError" in response:
            _error = response["writeConcernError"]
            _labels = response.get("errorLabels")
            if _labels:
                _error.update({"errorLabels": _labels})
            _raise_write_concern_error(_error)
    
        if response["ok"]:
            return
    
        details = response
        errmsg = details["errmsg"]
        code = details.get("code")
    
        # For allowable errors, only check for error messages when the code is not
        # included.
        if allowable_errors:
            if code is not None:
                if code in allowable_errors:
                    return
            elif errmsg in allowable_errors:
                return
    
        # Server is "not primary" or "recovering"
        if code is not None:
            if code in _NOT_PRIMARY_CODES:
                raise NotPrimaryError(errmsg, response)
        elif HelloCompat.LEGACY_ERROR in errmsg or "node is recovering" in errmsg:
            raise NotPrimaryError(errmsg, response)
    
        # Other errors
        # findAndModify with upsert can raise duplicate key error
        if code in (11000, 11001, 12582):
            raise DuplicateKeyError(errmsg, code, response, max_wire_version)
        elif code == 50:
            # Append timeout details to MaxTimeMSExpired responses.
            if pool_opts:
                timeout_details = _get_timeout_details(pool_opts)
                errmsg += format_timeout_details(timeout_details)
            raise ExecutionTimeout(errmsg, code, response, max_wire_version)
        elif code == 43:
            raise CursorNotFound(errmsg, code, response, max_wire_version)

    def _check_command_response(
        response: _DocumentOut,
        max_wire_version: Optional[int],
        allowable_errors: Optional[Container[Union[int, str]]] = None,
        parse_write_concern_error: bool = False,
        pool_opts: Optional[PoolOptions] = None,
    ) -> None:
        """Check the response to a command for errors."""
        if "ok" not in response:
            # Server didn't recognize our message as a command.
            raise OperationFailure(
                response.get("$err"),  # type: ignore[arg-type]
                response.get("code"),
                response,
                max_wire_version,
            )
    
        if parse_write_concern_error and "writeConcernError" in response:
            _error = response["writeConcernError"]
            _labels = response.get("errorLabels")
            if _labels:
                _error.update({"errorLabels": _labels})
            _raise_write_concern_error(_error)
    
        if response["ok"]:
            return
    
        details = response
        errmsg = details["errmsg"]
        code = details.get("code")
    
        # For allowable errors, only check for error messages when the code is not
        # included.
        if allowable_errors:
            if code is not None:
                if code in allowable_errors:
                    return
            elif errmsg in allowable_errors:
                return
    
        # Server is "not primary" or "recovering"
        if code is not None:
            if code in _NOT_PRIMARY_CODES:
                raise NotPrimaryError(errmsg, response)
        elif HelloCompat.LEGACY_ERROR in errmsg or "node is recovering" in errmsg:
            raise NotPrimaryError(errmsg, response)
    
        # Other errors
        # findAndModify with upsert can raise duplicate key error
        if code in (11000, 11001, 12582):
            raise DuplicateKeyError(errmsg, code, response, max_wire_version)
        elif code == 50:
            # Append timeout details to MaxTimeMSExpired responses.
            if pool_opts:
                timeout_details = _get_timeout_details(pool_opts)
                errmsg += format_timeout_details(timeout_details)
            raise ExecutionTimeout(errmsg, code, response, max_wire_version)
        elif code == 43:
            raise CursorNotFound(errmsg, code, response, max_wire_version)

max_wire_version = 21, allowable_errors = None, parse_write_concern_error = True
pool_opts = None

    def _check_command_response(
        response: _DocumentOut,
        max_wire_version: Optional[int],
        allowable_errors: Optional[Container[Union[int, str]]] = None,
        parse_write_concern_error: bool = False,
        pool_opts: Optional[PoolOptions] = None,
    ) -> None:
        """Check the response to a command for errors."""
        if "ok" not in response:
            # Server didn't recognize our message as a command.
            raise OperationFailure(
                response.get("$err"),  # type: ignore[arg-type]
                response.get("code"),
                response,
                max_wire_version,
            )
    
        if parse_write_concern_error and "writeConcernError" in response:
            _error = response["writeConcernError"]
            _labels = response.get("errorLabels")
            if _labels:
                _error.update({"errorLabels": _labels})
            _raise_write_concern_error(_error)
    
        if response["ok"]:
            return
    
        details = response
        errmsg = details["errmsg"]
        code = details.get("code")
    
        # For allowable errors, only check for error messages when the code is not
        # included.
        if allowable_errors:
            if code is not None:
                if code in allowable_errors:
                    return
            elif errmsg in allowable_errors:
                return
    
        # Server is "not primary" or "recovering"
        if code is not None:
            if code in _NOT_PRIMARY_CODES:
                raise NotPrimaryError(errmsg, response)
        elif HelloCompat.LEGACY_ERROR in errmsg or "node is recovering" in errmsg:
            raise NotPrimaryError(errmsg, response)
    
        # Other errors
        # findAndModify with upsert can raise duplicate key error
        if code in (11000, 11001, 12582):
            raise DuplicateKeyError(errmsg, code, response, max_wire_version)
        elif code == 50:
            # Append timeout details to MaxTimeMSExpired responses.
            if pool_opts:
                timeout_details = _get_timeout_details(pool_opts)
                errmsg += format_timeout_details(timeout_details)
            raise ExecutionTimeout(errmsg, code, response, max_wire_version)
        elif code == 43:
            raise CursorNotFound(errmsg, code, response, max_wire_version)

    def _check_command_response(
        response: _DocumentOut,
        max_wire_version: Optional[int],
        allowable_errors: Optional[Container[Union[int, str]]] = None,
        parse_write_concern_error: bool = False,
        pool_opts: Optional[PoolOptions] = None,
    ) -> None:
        """Check the response to a command for errors."""
        if "ok" not in response:
            # Server didn't recognize our message as a command.
            raise OperationFailure(
                response.get("$err"),  # type: ignore[arg-type]
                response.get("code"),
                response,
                max_wire_version,
            )
    
        if parse_write_concern_error and "writeConcernError" in response:
            _error = response["writeConcernError"]
            _labels = response.get("errorLabels")
            if _labels:
                _error.update({"errorLabels": _labels})
            _raise_write_concern_error(_error)
    
        if response["ok"]:
            return
    
        details = response
        errmsg = details["errmsg"]
        code = details.get("code")
    
        # For allowable errors, only check for error messages when the code is not
        # included.
        if allowable_errors:
            if code is not None:
                if code in allowable_errors:
                    return
            elif errmsg in allowable_errors:
                return
    
        # Server is "not primary" or "recovering"
        if code is not None:
            if code in _NOT_PRIMARY_CODES:
                raise NotPrimaryError(errmsg, response)
        elif HelloCompat.LEGACY_ERROR in errmsg or "node is recovering" in errmsg:
            raise NotPrimaryError(errmsg, response)
    
        # Other errors
        # findAndModify with upsert can raise duplicate key error
        if code in (11000, 11001, 12582):
            raise DuplicateKeyError(errmsg, code, response, max_wire_version)
        elif code == 50:
            # Append timeout details to MaxTimeMSExpired responses.
            if pool_opts:
                timeout_details = _get_timeout_details(pool_opts)
                errmsg += format_timeout_details(timeout_details)
            raise ExecutionTimeout(errmsg, code, response, max_wire_version)
        elif code == 43:
            raise CursorNotFound(errmsg, code, response, max_wire_version)

    def _check_command_response(
        response: _DocumentOut,
        max_wire_version: Optional[int],
        allowable_errors: Optional[Container[Union[int, str]]] = None,
        parse_write_concern_error: bool = False,
        pool_opts: Optional[PoolOptions] = None,
    ) -> None:
        """Check the response to a command for errors."""
        if "ok" not in response:
            # Server didn't recognize our message as a command.
            raise OperationFailure(
                response.get("$err"),  # type: ignore[arg-type]
                response.get("code"),
                response,
                max_wire_version,
            )
    
        if parse_write_concern_error and "writeConcernError" in response:
            _error = response["writeConcernError"]
            _labels = response.get("errorLabels")
            if _labels:
                _error.update({"errorLabels": _labels})
            _raise_write_concern_error(_error)
    
        if response["ok"]:
            return
    
        details = response
        errmsg = details["errmsg"]
        code = details.get("code")
    
        # For allowable errors, only check for error messages when the code is not
        # included.
        if allowable_errors:
            if code is not None:
                if code in allowable_errors:
                    return
            elif errmsg in allowable_errors:
                return
    
        # Server is "not primary" or "recovering"
        if code is not None:
            if code in _NOT_PRIMARY_CODES:
                raise NotPrimaryError(errmsg, response)
        elif HelloCompat.LEGACY_ERROR in errmsg or "node is recovering" in errmsg:
            raise NotPrimaryError(errmsg, response)
    
        # Other errors
        # findAndModify with upsert can raise duplicate key error
        if code in (11000, 11001, 12582):
            raise DuplicateKeyError(errmsg, code, response, max_wire_version)
        elif code == 50:
            # Append timeout details to MaxTimeMSExpired responses.
            if pool_opts:
                timeout_details = _get_timeout_details(pool_opts)
                errmsg += format_timeout_details(timeout_details)
            raise ExecutionTimeout(errmsg, code, response, max_wire_version)
        elif code == 43:
            raise CursorNotFound(errmsg, code, response, max_wire_version)

    def _check_command_response(
        response: _DocumentOut,
        max_wire_version: Optional[int],
        allowable_errors: Optional[Container[Union[int, str]]] = None,
        parse_write_concern_error: bool = False,
        pool_opts: Optional[PoolOptions] = None,
    ) -> None:
        """Check the response to a command for errors."""
        if "ok" not in response:
            # Server didn't recognize our message as a command.
            raise OperationFailure(
                response.get("$err"),  # type: ignore[arg-type]
                response.get("code"),
                response,
                max_wire_version,
            )
    
        if parse_write_concern_error and "writeConcernError" in response:
            _error = response["writeConcernError"]
            _labels = response.get("errorLabels")
            if _labels:
                _error.update({"errorLabels": _labels})
            _raise_write_concern_error(_error)
    
        if response["ok"]:
            return
    
        details = response
        errmsg = details["errmsg"]
        code = details.get("code")
    
        # For allowable errors, only check for error messages when the code is not
        # included.
        if allowable_errors:
            if code is not None:
                if code in allowable_errors:
                    return
            elif errmsg in allowable_errors:
                return
    
        # Server is "not primary" or "recovering"
        if code is not None:
            if code in _NOT_PRIMARY_CODES:
                raise NotPrimaryError(errmsg, response)
        elif HelloCompat.LEGACY_ERROR in errmsg or "node is recovering" in errmsg:
            raise NotPrimaryError(errmsg, response)
    
        # Other errors
        # findAndModify with upsert can raise duplicate key error
        if code in (11000, 11001, 12582):
            raise DuplicateKeyError(errmsg, code, response, max_wire_version)
        elif code == 50:
            # Append timeout details to MaxTimeMSExpired responses.
            if pool_opts:
                timeout_details = _get_timeout_details(pool_opts)
                errmsg += format_timeout_details(timeout_details)
            raise ExecutionTimeout(errmsg, code, response, max_wire_version)
        elif code == 43:
            raise CursorNotFound(errmsg, code, response, max_wire_version)

max_wire_version = 21, allowable_errors = None, parse_write_concern_error = True
pool_opts = None

    def _check_command_response(
        response: _DocumentOut,
        max_wire_version: Optional[int],
        allowable_errors: Optional[Container[Union[int, str]]] = None,
        parse_write_concern_error: bool = False,
        pool_opts: Optional[PoolOptions] = None,
    ) -> None:
        """Check the response to a command for errors."""
        if "ok" not in response:
            # Server didn't recognize our message as a command.
            raise OperationFailure(
                response.get("$err"),  # type: ignore[arg-type]
                response.get("code"),
                response,
                max_wire_version,
            )
    
        if parse_write_concern_error and "writeConcernError" in response:
            _error = response["writeConcernError"]
            _labels = response.get("errorLabels")
            if _labels:
                _error.update({"errorLabels": _labels})
            _raise_write_concern_error(_error)
    
        if response["ok"]:
            return
    
        details = response
        errmsg = details["errmsg"]
        code = details.get("code")
    
        # For allowable errors, only check for error messages when the code is not
        # included.
        if allowable_errors:
            if code is not None:
                if code in allowable_errors:
                    return
            elif errmsg in allowable_errors:
                return
    
        # Server is "not primary" or "recovering"
        if code is not None:
            if code in _NOT_PRIMARY_CODES:
                raise NotPrimaryError(errmsg, response)
        elif HelloCompat.LEGACY_ERROR in errmsg or "node is recovering" in errmsg:
            raise NotPrimaryError(errmsg, response)
    
        # Other errors
        # findAndModify with upsert can raise duplicate key error
        if code in (11000, 11001, 12582):
            raise DuplicateKeyError(errmsg, code, response, max_wire_version)
        elif code == 50:
            # Append timeout details to MaxTimeMSExpired responses.
            if pool_opts:
                timeout_details = _get_timeout_details(pool_opts)
                errmsg += format_timeout_details(timeout_details)
            raise ExecutionTimeout(errmsg, code, response, max_wire_version)
        elif code == 43:
            raise CursorNotFound(errmsg, code, response, max_wire_version)

    def _check_command_response(
        response: _DocumentOut,
        max_wire_version: Optional[int],
        allowable_errors: Optional[Container[Union[int, str]]] = None,
        parse_write_concern_error: bool = False,
        pool_opts: Optional[PoolOptions] = None,
    ) -> None:
        """Check the response to a command for errors."""
        if "ok" not in response:
            # Server didn't recognize our message as a command.
            raise OperationFailure(
                response.get("$err"),  # type: ignore[arg-type]
                response.get("code"),
                response,
                max_wire_version,
            )
    
        if parse_write_concern_error and "writeConcernError" in response:
            _error = response["writeConcernError"]
            _labels = response.get("errorLabels")
            if _labels:
                _error.update({"errorLabels": _labels})
            _raise_write_concern_error(_error)
    
        if response["ok"]:
            return
    
        details = response
        errmsg = details["errmsg"]
        code = details.get("code")
    
        # For allowable errors, only check for error messages when the code is not
        # included.
        if allowable_errors:
            if code is not None:
                if code in allowable_errors:
                    return
            elif errmsg in allowable_errors:
                return
    
        # Server is "not primary" or "recovering"
        if code is not None:
            if code in _NOT_PRIMARY_CODES:
                raise NotPrimaryError(errmsg, response)
        elif HelloCompat.LEGACY_ERROR in errmsg or "node is recovering" in errmsg:
            raise NotPrimaryError(errmsg, response)
    
        # Other errors
        # findAndModify with upsert can raise duplicate key error
        if code in (11000, 11001, 12582):
            raise DuplicateKeyError(errmsg, code, response, max_wire_version)
        elif code == 50:
            # Append timeout details to MaxTimeMSExpired responses.
            if pool_opts:
                timeout_details = _get_timeout_details(pool_opts)
                errmsg += format_timeout_details(timeout_details)
            raise ExecutionTimeout(errmsg, code, response, max_wire_version)
        elif code == 43:
            raise CursorNotFound(errmsg, code, response, max_wire_version)

    def _check_command_response(
        response: _DocumentOut,
        max_wire_version: Optional[int],
        allowable_errors: Optional[Container[Union[int, str]]] = None,
        parse_write_concern_error: bool = False,
        pool_opts: Optional[PoolOptions] = None,
    ) -> None:
        """Check the response to a command for errors."""
        if "ok" not in response:
            # Server didn't recognize our message as a command.
            raise OperationFailure(
                response.get("$err"),  # type: ignore[arg-type]
                response.get("code"),
                response,
                max_wire_version,
            )
    
        if parse_write_concern_error and "writeConcernError" in response:
            _error = response["writeConcernError"]
            _labels = response.get("errorLabels")
            if _labels:
                _error.update({"errorLabels": _labels})
            _raise_write_concern_error(_error)
    
        if response["ok"]:
            return
    
        details = response
        errmsg = details["errmsg"]
        code = details.get("code")
    
        # For allowable errors, only check for error messages when the code is not
        # included.
        if allowable_errors:
            if code is not None:
                if code in allowable_errors:
                    return
            elif errmsg in allowable_errors:
                return
    
        # Server is "not primary" or "recovering"
        if code is not None:
            if code in _NOT_PRIMARY_CODES:
                raise NotPrimaryError(errmsg, response)
        elif HelloCompat.LEGACY_ERROR in errmsg or "node is recovering" in errmsg:
            raise NotPrimaryError(errmsg, response)
    
        # Other errors
        # findAndModify with upsert can raise duplicate key error
        if code in (11000, 11001, 12582):
            raise DuplicateKeyError(errmsg, code, response, max_wire_version)
        elif code == 50:
            # Append timeout details to MaxTimeMSExpired responses.
            if pool_opts:
                timeout_details = _get_timeout_details(pool_opts)
                errmsg += format_timeout_details(timeout_details)
            raise ExecutionTimeout(errmsg, code, response, max_wire_version)
        elif code == 43:
            raise CursorNotFound(errmsg, code, response, max_wire_version)

____________________ test_kill9_while_the_governor_flushes _____________________

    def test_kill9_while_the_governor_flushes():
        """Rule 8 with the governor at work all the time: `--memtables-total-mb 1` (budget 1 MiB, writes wait past 1.5 MiB),
        24 writers over 8 databases with documents of 100 B to 20 KB, kill -9 at a random moment. Every acknowledged
        document is there after a restart, with its value; the governor's counters show it flushed and held writes."""
        bucket = make_bucket("t82g")
        s = Server(bucket, "t82g", idle=600, extra=["--memtables-total-mb", "1"]).start()
        acked, stop = [], threading.Event()
    
        def work(t):
            from pymongo import MongoClient
            c = MongoClient(s.uri, serverSelectionTimeoutMS=5000, socketTimeoutMS=30000)
            k = 0
            while not stop.is_set():
                try:
                    c[f"g{t % 8}"].t.insert_one({"_id": f"{t}-{k}", "v": k, "pad": "x" * random.randint(100, 20000)})
                except PyMongoError:
                    return
                acked.append((f"g{t % 8}", f"{t}-{k}", k))
                k += 1
    
        ts = [threading.Thread(target=work, args=(t,)) for t in range(24)]
        for t in ts:
            t.start()
        time.sleep(random.uniform(8, 14))
        mb = s.client().admin.command("serverStatus")["fenvix"]["memoryBudget"]
        s.kill9()
        stop.set()
        for t in ts:
            t.join(60)
        print(f"T82G before the kill: acknowledged {len(acked)}; memtables peak {mb['memtablesPeak'] >> 10} KiB (budget {mb['memtablesBytes'] >> 10}, "
              f"hard {mb['memtablesHardBytes'] >> 10}), budget flushes {mb['memtableBudgetFlushes']}, write waits {mb['memtableBudgetWaits']}, "
              f"wait timeouts {mb['memtableBudgetWaitTimeouts']}", flush=True)
        r = Server(bucket, "t82gr", idle=600, extra=["--memtables-total-mb", "1"]).start()
        from pymongo import MongoClient
        c = MongoClient(r.uri, serverSelectionTimeoutMS=30000, socketTimeoutMS=300000)
        by = {}
        for d, i, v in acked:
            by.setdefault(d, {})[i] = v
        lost, wrong = [], []
        for d, want in by.items():
            for attempt in range(120):
                try:
                    got = {x["_id"]: x["v"] for x in c[d].t.find({}, {"v": 1})}
                    break
                except OperationFailure as e:
                    if e.code != 134 or attempt == 119:
                        raise
                    time.sleep(0.5)
            lost += [i for i in want if i not in got]
            wrong += [i for i, v in want.items() if i in got and got[i] != v]
        print(f"T82G after the restart: {len(acked)} acknowledged documents checked, lost {len(lost)}, wrong {len(wrong)}", flush=True)
        r.term()
        assert not lost and not wrong, (lost[:10], wrong[:10])
>       assert mb["memtableBudgetFlushes"] > 0, "the governor never flushed: the test did not exercise it"

    def _check_command_response(
        response: _DocumentOut,
        max_wire_version: Optional[int],
        allowable_errors: Optional[Container[Union[int, str]]] = None,
        parse_write_concern_error: bool = False,
        pool_opts: Optional[PoolOptions] = None,
    ) -> None:
        """Check the response to a command for errors."""
        if "ok" not in response:
            # Server didn't recognize our message as a command.
            raise OperationFailure(
                response.get("$err"),  # type: ignore[arg-type]
                response.get("code"),
                response,
                max_wire_version,
            )
    
        if parse_write_concern_error and "writeConcernError" in response:
            _error = response["writeConcernError"]
            _labels = response.get("errorLabels")
            if _labels:
                _error.update({"errorLabels": _labels})
            _raise_write_concern_error(_error)
    
        if response["ok"]:
            return
    
        details = response
        errmsg = details["errmsg"]
        code = details.get("code")
    
        # For allowable errors, only check for error messages when the code is not
        # included.
        if allowable_errors:
            if code is not None:
                if code in allowable_errors:
                    return
            elif errmsg in allowable_errors:
                return
    
        # Server is "not primary" or "recovering"
        if code is not None:
            if code in _NOT_PRIMARY_CODES:
                raise NotPrimaryError(errmsg, response)
        elif HelloCompat.LEGACY_ERROR in errmsg or "node is recovering" in errmsg:
            raise NotPrimaryError(errmsg, response)
    
        # Other errors
        # findAndModify with upsert can raise duplicate key error
        if code in (11000, 11001, 12582):
            raise DuplicateKeyError(errmsg, code, response, max_wire_version)
        elif code == 50:
            # Append timeout details to MaxTimeMSExpired responses.
            if pool_opts:
                timeout_details = _get_timeout_details(pool_opts)
                errmsg += format_timeout_details(timeout_details)
            raise ExecutionTimeout(errmsg, code, response, max_wire_version)
        elif code == 43:
            raise CursorNotFound(errmsg, code, response, max_wire_version)

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("span", [True, False])
    def test_span_scan_of_a_customers_documents(span):
        """A customer's documents read by one span scan give the same answers as one point read each (control: the scan

max_wire_version = 21, allowable_errors = None, parse_write_concern_error = True
pool_opts = None

    def _check_command_response(
        response: _DocumentOut,
        max_wire_version: Optional[int],
        allowable_errors: Optional[Container[Union[int, str]]] = None,
        parse_write_concern_error: bool = False,
        pool_opts: Optional[PoolOptions] = None,
    ) -> None:
        """Check the response to a command for errors."""
        if "ok" not in response:
            # Server didn't recognize our message as a command.
            raise OperationFailure(
                response.get("$err"),  # type: ignore[arg-type]
                response.get("code"),
                response,
                max_wire_version,
            )
    
        if parse_write_concern_error and "writeConcernError" in response:
            _error = response["writeConcernError"]
            _labels = response.get("errorLabels")
            if _labels:
                _error.update({"errorLabels": _labels})
            _raise_write_concern_error(_error)
    
        if response["ok"]:
            return
    
        details = response
        errmsg = details["errmsg"]
        code = details.get("code")
    
        # For allowable errors, only check for error messages when the code is not
        # included.
        if allowable_errors:
            if code is not None:
                if code in allowable_errors:
                    return
            elif errmsg in allowable_errors:
                return
    
        # Server is "not primary" or "recovering"
        if code is not None:
            if code in _NOT_PRIMARY_CODES:
                raise NotPrimaryError(errmsg, response)
        elif HelloCompat.LEGACY_ERROR in errmsg or "node is recovering" in errmsg:
            raise NotPrimaryError(errmsg, response)
    
        # Other errors
        # findAndModify with upsert can raise duplicate key error
        if code in (11000, 11001, 12582):
            raise DuplicateKeyError(errmsg, code, response, max_wire_version)
        elif code == 50:
            # Append timeout details to MaxTimeMSExpired responses.
            if pool_opts:
                timeout_details = _get_timeout_details(pool_opts)
                errmsg += format_timeout_details(timeout_details)
            raise ExecutionTimeout(errmsg, code, response, max_wire_version)
        elif code == 43:
            raise CursorNotFound(errmsg, code, response, max_wire_version)

    def _check_command_response(
        response: _DocumentOut,
        max_wire_version: Optional[int],
        allowable_errors: Optional[Container[Union[int, str]]] = None,
        parse_write_concern_error: bool = False,
        pool_opts: Optional[PoolOptions] = None,
    ) -> None:
        """Check the response to a command for errors."""
        if "ok" not in response:
            # Server didn't recognize our message as a command.
            raise OperationFailure(
                response.get("$err"),  # type: ignore[arg-type]
                response.get("code"),
                response,
                max_wire_version,
            )
    
        if parse_write_concern_error and "writeConcernError" in response:
            _error = response["writeConcernError"]
            _labels = response.get("errorLabels")
            if _labels:
                _error.update({"errorLabels": _labels})
            _raise_write_concern_error(_error)
    
        if response["ok"]:
            return
    
        details = response
        errmsg = details["errmsg"]
        code = details.get("code")
    
        # For allowable errors, only check for error messages when the code is not
        # included.
        if allowable_errors:
            if code is not None:
                if code in allowable_errors:
                    return
            elif errmsg in allowable_errors:
                return
    
        # Server is "not primary" or "recovering"
        if code is not None:
            if code in _NOT_PRIMARY_CODES:
                raise NotPrimaryError(errmsg, response)
        elif HelloCompat.LEGACY_ERROR in errmsg or "node is recovering" in errmsg:
            raise NotPrimaryError(errmsg, response)
    
        # Other errors
        # findAndModify with upsert can raise duplicate key error
        if code in (11000, 11001, 12582):
            raise DuplicateKeyError(errmsg, code, response, max_wire_version)
        elif code == 50:
            # Append timeout details to MaxTimeMSExpired responses.
            if pool_opts:
                timeout_details = _get_timeout_details(pool_opts)
                errmsg += format_timeout_details(timeout_details)
            raise ExecutionTimeout(errmsg, code, response, max_wire_version)
        elif code == 43:
            raise CursorNotFound(errmsg, code, response, max_wire_version)

    def _check_command_response(
        response: _DocumentOut,
        max_wire_version: Optional[int],
        allowable_errors: Optional[Container[Union[int, str]]] = None,
        parse_write_concern_error: bool = False,
        pool_opts: Optional[PoolOptions] = None,
    ) -> None:
        """Check the response to a command for errors."""
        if "ok" not in response:
            # Server didn't recognize our message as a command.
            raise OperationFailure(
                response.get("$err"),  # type: ignore[arg-type]
                response.get("code"),
                response,
                max_wire_version,
            )
    
        if parse_write_concern_error and "writeConcernError" in response:
            _error = response["writeConcernError"]
            _labels = response.get("errorLabels")
            if _labels:
                _error.update({"errorLabels": _labels})
            _raise_write_concern_error(_error)
    
        if response["ok"]:
            return
    
        details = response
        errmsg = details["errmsg"]
        code = details.get("code")
    
        # For allowable errors, only check for error messages when the code is not
        # included.
        if allowable_errors:
            if code is not None:
                if code in allowable_errors:
                    return
            elif errmsg in allowable_errors:
                return
    
        # Server is "not primary" or "recovering"
        if code is not None:
            if code in _NOT_PRIMARY_CODES:
                raise NotPrimaryError(errmsg, response)
        elif HelloCompat.LEGACY_ERROR in errmsg or "node is recovering" in errmsg:
            raise NotPrimaryError(errmsg, response)
    
        # Other errors
        # findAndModify with upsert can raise duplicate key error
        if code in (11000, 11001, 12582):
            raise DuplicateKeyError(errmsg, code, response, max_wire_version)
        elif code == 50:
            # Append timeout details to MaxTimeMSExpired responses.
            if pool_opts:
                timeout_details = _get_timeout_details(pool_opts)
                errmsg += format_timeout_details(timeout_details)
            raise ExecutionTimeout(errmsg, code, response, max_wire_version)
        elif code == 43:
            raise CursorNotFound(errmsg, code, response, max_wire_version)

    def _check_command_response(
        response: _DocumentOut,
        max_wire_version: Optional[int],
        allowable_errors: Optional[Container[Union[int, str]]] = None,
        parse_write_concern_error: bool = False,
        pool_opts: Optional[PoolOptions] = None,
    ) -> None:
        """Check the response to a command for errors."""
        if "ok" not in response:
            # Server didn't recognize our message as a command.
            raise OperationFailure(
                response.get("$err"),  # type: ignore[arg-type]
                response.get("code"),
                response,
                max_wire_version,
            )
    
        if parse_write_concern_error and "writeConcernError" in response:
            _error = response["writeConcernError"]
            _labels = response.get("errorLabels")
            if _labels:
                _error.update({"errorLabels": _labels})
            _raise_write_concern_error(_error)
    
        if response["ok"]:
            return
    
        details = response
        errmsg = details["errmsg"]
        code = details.get("code")
    
        # For allowable errors, only check for error messages when the code is not
        # included.
        if allowable_errors:
            if code is not None:
                if code in allowable_errors:
                    return
            elif errmsg in allowable_errors:
                return
    
        # Server is "not primary" or "recovering"
        if code is not None:
            if code in _NOT_PRIMARY_CODES:
                raise NotPrimaryError(errmsg, response)
        elif HelloCompat.LEGACY_ERROR in errmsg or "node is recovering" in errmsg:
            raise NotPrimaryError(errmsg, response)
    
        # Other errors
        # findAndModify with upsert can raise duplicate key error
        if code in (11000, 11001, 12582):
            raise DuplicateKeyError(errmsg, code, response, max_wire_version)
        elif code == 50:
            # Append timeout details to MaxTimeMSExpired responses.
            if pool_opts:
                timeout_details = _get_timeout_details(pool_opts)
                errmsg += format_timeout_details(timeout_details)
            raise ExecutionTimeout(errmsg, code, response, max_wire_version)
        elif code == 43:
            raise CursorNotFound(errmsg, code, response, max_wire_version)
